# Skills · I · part 104

866 entries, issue-checker to issue-linked-git-workflow. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **issue-checker**: Verifies whether a claimed issue is real and/or still present. Bidirectional: use it to validate that a reported bug, finding, or vulnerability actually exists before fixing it (avoid wasted work on phantom issues), and to confirm that a fix truly resolved an issue afterwards (avoid declaring victory on a fix that didn't land). Each claim gets a verdict: PRESENT, NOT-PRESENT, or INCONCLUSIVE: backed by reproducible evidence (code citations, command output, test result), never by "looks fine" or "looks fixed". Use when the user asks to verify a bug report, double-check a fix, audit FIND-xxx it… ([alexanderyoza/alexanderyoza](https://github.com/alexanderyoza/alexanderyoza/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/issue-checker))
